        ### Task Summary
        Implement a two-tier shot group system on the video editor timeline. The current `useShotGroups` inference becomes "suggested" groups (dashed border). Users can promote these to "pinned" groups persisted in TimelineConfig, enabling grouped drag, image↔video toggle with snapshot/restore, auto-sync with shot image changes, and context menu actions (Pin/Unpin, Switch to Video/Images). PinnedShotGroup data must survive save/load cycles and integrate with the undo system.

        ### Key Evidence
        - TimelineConfig has strict serialization validation: ALLOWED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS in serialize.ts:98 only allows 'output', 'clips', 'tracks', 'customEffects'. Adding pinnedShotGroups requires updating this set, serializeForDisk(), and validateSerializedConfig(). (source: src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.ts:98-122; relevance: high)
- TimelineData is the runtime type holding config, rows, meta, registry. pinnedShotGroups should be added to TimelineData AND TimelineConfig for persistence. Config goes through serializeForDisk → saveTimeline → loadTimeline → buildTimelineData round-trip. (source: src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts:61-75; relevance: high)
- useShotGroups currently infers groups from clip adjacency, generation ID matching against shot images, and a 0.5s MAX_GAP threshold. Returns ShotGroup with shotId, shotName, rowId, rowIndex, clipIds, color. This becomes the 'suggested' tier. (source: src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.ts:39-140; relevance: high)
- useSwitchToFinalVideo replaces image clips with a single video clip in one applyEdit call. It does NOT snapshot the original clips for swap-back. The pinned group system needs to snapshot clipIds+meta before swap and restore on switch-back. (source: src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts:23-107; relevance: high)
- Clip drag supports multi-select via DragSession.draggedClipIds with offset-based movement. Grouped drag for pinned groups can leverage this existing multi-drag infrastructure (planMultiDragMoves, applyMultiDragMoves). (source: src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts:50-74,381-449; relevance: high)
- ShotGroupContextMenu currently has ShotGroupMenuState with x,y,shotId,shotName,clipIds,rowId,hasFinalVideo. Needs 'isPinned' field added for Pin/Unpin toggle and mode-aware menu items. (source: src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ShotGroupContextMenu.tsx:4-12; relevance: high)
- Shot group rendering in TimelineCanvas uses PositionedShotGroup with pixel coordinates. Visual distinction (dashed vs solid border) applies here. The border is currently set as a plain 'border' class at line 769. (source: src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx:758-769; relevance: high)
- External drop flow (useExternalDrop) dispatches generation drops through handleAssetDrop which creates clips on tracks. Shot drops from shot list need a new handler that creates multiple image clips as a pinned group. (source: src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts:259-299; relevance: medium)
- ShotsContext provides shots with images array. useShotImages/useAllShotGenerations loads shot_generations with React Query + realtimeBacked preset. Pinned group auto-sync can subscribe to shot image changes via these hooks. (source: src/shared/contexts/ShotsContext.tsx:6-14; relevance: medium)
- applyEdit accepts TimelineEditMutation with type 'rows' (row/meta changes) or 'config' (full config rebuild). Pinned group mutations that only change pinnedShotGroups metadata (not clips) could use a lightweight path, but clip-affecting operations need full applyEdit. (source: src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts:46-57; relevance: medium)
- Existing tests in useShotGroups.test.ts cover inference logic with 7 cases. These must continue passing (suggested groups unchanged). New tests needed for pinned group suppression of inferred groups for same shot+track. (source: src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.test.ts:39-148; relevance: medium)

        ### Constraints
        - All 8 existing useShotGroups tests must continue to pass unchanged
- Timeline config serialization must handle pinnedShotGroups in ALLOWED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS, serializeForDisk, and validateSerializedConfig
- Pinned groups must survive save/load/conflict-retry round-trips (DataProvider.saveTimeline → loadTimeline)
- Undo/redo must work with pinned group operations since they go through applyEdit
- useShotGroups inference must suppress inferred groups when a pinned group exists for the same shot+track
- Switch-to-video must snapshot image clips in PinnedShotGroup.imageClipSnapshot before swapping, so switch-back restores them
- Grouped drag must use existing multi-drag infrastructure (planMultiDragMoves/applyMultiDragMoves), not a separate drag system
- No hardcoded colors — dashed/solid border distinction must use existing color-mix pattern from TimelineCanvas
- Image↔video toggle must be a single applyEdit call in each direction for clean undo

        ### Suggested Approach
        **Phase 1: Data model** — Add `PinnedShotGroup` type and `pinnedShotGroups?: PinnedShotGroup[]` to `TimelineConfig` (types/index.ts), `TimelineData` (timeline-data.ts). Update serialize.ts: add to `ALLOWED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS`, pass through in `serializeForDisk`, allow in `validateSerializedConfig`. Thread through `assembleTimelineData`, `buildTimelineData`, `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry`, and `rowsToConfig`.

**Phase 2: Inference suppression** — Modify `useShotGroups` to accept `pinnedShotGroups` param. Build a Set of `${shotId}:${trackId}` keys from pinned groups. Skip inferred groups matching those keys. Add `isPinned: boolean` to `ShotGroup` interface so downstream code can distinguish. Return merged array: pinned groups (from config) + filtered inferred groups.

**Phase 3: Visual distinction** — Add `isPinned` to `PositionedShotGroup` in TimelineCanvas. Render dashed border (`border-dashed`) for suggested, solid for pinned. Show shot name label always for pinned (not just on hover). Keep existing color-mix pattern.

**Phase 4: Pin/Unpin via context menu** — Add `isPinned` to `ShotGroupMenuState`. Add "Pin as Shot Group" / "Unpin" menu items. Pin action: read current clipIds from the inferred group, write `PinnedShotGroup` to config via `applyEdit`. Unpin: remove from config via `applyEdit`. Create a `usePinnedShotGroups` hook encapsulating pin/unpin/update mutations.

**Phase 5: Switch-to-video refactor** — Modify `useSwitchToFinalVideo` to auto-pin first if not already pinned. Before swapping, snapshot image clip metas into `PinnedShotGroup.imageClipSnapshot`. On switch-back, restore from snapshot. Both directions as single `applyEdit` calls.

**Phase 6: Grouped drag** — In `useClipDrag`, on pointer-down check if clip belongs to a pinned group. If so, auto-select all pinned group clipIds into `draggedClipIds`. This leverages existing multi-drag path with `planMultiDragMoves`/`applyMultiDragMoves`.

**Phase 7: Shot drop** — In `useExternalDrop`, detect shot-type drag data. On drop, create image clips for all shot images using `handleAssetDrop` pattern, then write a `PinnedShotGroup` entry. Single `applyEdit` for atomic creation.

**Phase 8: Auto-sync** — Subscribe to `ShotsContext` changes. When images added/removed from a shot that has a pinned group, update the pinned group's clipIds and add/remove corresponding timeline clips via `applyEdit`.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Implement two-tier shot groups on the video editor timeline: suggested (inferred, dashed border) and pinned (persistent, solid border).

## Core concept
The current useShotGroups inference becomes 'suggested' groups shown with dashed borders. Users can promote them to 'pinned' groups which are stored in timeline config, persist across refresh, and enable advanced features.

## Data model
PinnedShotGroup stored in timelineConfig:
- shotId, trackId, clipIds[], mode: 'images' | 'video', videoAssetKey?, imageClipSnapshot? (for swap-back)

## When pinned groups are created (explicit + implicit):

### Explicit:
1. Right-click a suggested group → 'Pin as Shot Group' - writes PinnedShotGroup to config
2. Drop a shot from shot list/gallery onto timeline - creates pinned group with images expanded as clips

### Implicit (auto-pin):
3. 'Switch to Final Video' on a suggested group - auto-pins first (snapshots image clips), then swaps to video mode
4. When an image is added to a shot that already has a pinned group on the timeline - auto-inserts the new image clip into the pinned group's track at the right position

## Visual distinction:
- Suggested: dashed border, lighter color (current border style → dashed)
- Pinned: solid border, full color (current styling), shot name visible

## Pinned group behavior:
- Clips drag together as a unit (grouped drag)
- Image ↔ video toggle: switch stores snapshot of image clips, restores on switch-back. Single applyEdit each direction.
- Shot image sync: when images are added/removed from a shot, pinned group auto-updates (subscribe to shot changes via realtime or ShotsContext)
- Context menu: Pin/Unpin, Switch to Video/Images, Jump to Shot, Generate Video

## Key architectural decisions:
- pinnedShotGroups stored in TimelineData (persisted via timeline config, undoable)
- useShotGroups still runs for inference but pinned groups take priority (suppress inferred for same shot+track)
- PinnedShotGroup clipIds are the source of truth for which clips belong to the group
- When a pinned group exists, the shot group overlay reads from the pinned data, not from inference

## Key files to research:
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.ts - current inference
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts - current swap (to be replaced)
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts - applyEdit, TimelineEditMutation, timeline config persistence
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts - drag behavior (need grouped drag)
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts - drop from outside (need shot drop)
- src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts - TimelineData type, ClipMeta
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - AssetRegistry types
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx - rendering shot groups
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ShotGroupContextMenu.tsx - context menu
- src/shared/contexts/ShotsContext.tsx - shot data, realtime updates
- src/shared/hooks/shots/useShotImages.ts - shot image data
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useAssetManagement.ts - asset registration
- src/tools/video-editor/contexts/TimelineEditorContext.ts - editor context/providers

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Two-Tier Shot Groups (Suggested + Pinned)

## Overview

Add a two-tier shot group system to the video editor timeline. The existing `useShotGroups` inference becomes "suggested" groups (dashed border). Users can promote these to "pinned" groups persisted in `TimelineConfig`, enabling grouped drag, image↔video toggle with snapshot/restore, auto-sync with shot image changes, and context menu actions.

**Critical architectural constraint:** The edit/commit cycle reconstructs `TimelineData` from scratch via `rowsToConfig → buildDataFromCurrentRegistry → assembleTimelineData`. All four functions explicitly pick fields — no spread operator preserves extras. So `pinnedShotGroups` must be threaded through every function in this pipeline, similar to how `customEffects` is handled.

## Phase 1: Data Model & Persistence

### Step 1: Define PinnedShotGroup type and add to TimelineConfig (`src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `PinnedShotGroup` interface:
   ```typescript
   export interface PinnedShotGroup {
     shotId: string;
     trackId: string;
     clipIds: string[];
     mode: 'images' | 'video';
     videoAssetKey?: string;
     imageClipSnapshot?: Array<{ clipId: string; meta: ClipMeta; assetKey: string }>;
   }
   ```
2. **Add** optional `pinnedShotGroups?: PinnedShotGroup[]` to `TimelineConfig` type (line ~146).

### Step 2: Thread pinnedShotGroups through serialization (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `'pinnedShotGroups'` to `ALLOWED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS` set (line 98).
2. **Update** `serializeForDisk` (line ~131) to include `pinnedShotGroups` in the serialized output if present — follow the `customEffects` pattern (conditional inclusion).
3. **Note:** `validateSerializedConfig` only checks top-level keys against `ALLOWED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS`, so step 1 handles validation.

### Step 3: Thread pinnedShotGroups through data assembly (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** `rowsToConfig` (line ~300): Accept `pinnedShotGroups` parameter. Include it in the returned `TimelineConfig` if non-empty (same pattern as `customEffects`).
2. **Update** `assembleTimelineData` (line ~319): No change needed — it receives the full `config` object which now includes `pinnedShotGroups`. The field flows through `config` naturally.
3. **Update** `configToRows` if needed — though `pinnedShotGroups` is metadata-only (not clip data), so it likely passes through config untouched.

### Step 4: Thread through commit cycle (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-save-utils.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** `materializeData` (line ~114): Pass `current.config.pinnedShotGroups` into the `rowsToConfig` call so it survives the rows→config→data round-trip.
2. **Update** `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry` (line ~23): The `config` parameter already has `pinnedShotGroups` as part of `TimelineConfig`. Verify `repairConfig` and `migrateToFlatTracks` don't strip unknown keys. If they do, pass `pinnedShotGroups` through explicitly.
3. **Update** `serializeForDisk` call in the save pipeline: Ensure `pinnedShotGroups` from config is passed through. The function now handles this from Step 2.

### Step 5: Verify persistence round-trip
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** existing serialization tests: `src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.test.ts` — must pass.
2. **Add** a test case that includes `pinnedShotGroups` in a config and verifies it survives `serializeForDisk → validateSerializedConfig` round-trip.
3. **Check** AI timeline agent compatibility: verify `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/` doesn't break on unknown config keys (it should ignore them, but confirm).

## Phase 2: Inference Suppression & Visual Distinction

### Step 6: Update useShotGroups to support pinned groups (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `isPinned: boolean` field to `ShotGroup` interface (line ~10).
2. **Add** `pinnedShotGroups?: PinnedShotGroup[]` parameter to `useShotGroups` function signature (line ~39).
3. **Build** a suppression Set of `${shotId}:${trackId}` keys from pinned groups.
4. **Convert** each `PinnedShotGroup` into a `ShotGroup` with `isPinned: true`, using `getShotColor(shotId)` for color and resolving `shotName` from shots array.
5. **Filter** inferred groups: skip any group whose `${shotId}:${trackId}` matches a pinned key.
6. **Return** merged array: pinned ShotGroups + filtered inferred ShotGroups (all inferred have `isPinned: false`).
7. **Verify** all 8 existing tests pass unchanged (they don't pass `pinnedShotGroups`, so parameter defaults to `undefined` and behavior is identical).

### Step 7: Wire pinnedShotGroups from TimelineData into useShotGroups (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `useShotGroups` call (line ~327) to pass `data?.config.pinnedShotGroups` as the new parameter.

### Step 8: Visual distinction in TimelineCanvas (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `isPinned: boolean` to `PositionedShotGroup` interface (line ~60).
2. **Populate** `isPinned` from `ShotGroup.isPinned` in the `positionedShotGroups` useMemo (line ~415).
3. **Update** shot group border rendering (line ~758-769):
   - Suggested: `border-dashed` with current color-mix opacity.
   - Pinned: `border-solid` (current default) with full color.
4. **Show** shot name label always for pinned groups (not just on hover). For suggested groups, keep hover behavior.

## Phase 3: Context Menu & Pin/Unpin

### Step 9: Create usePinnedShotGroups hook (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** hook that encapsulates pin/unpin mutations:
   - `pinGroup(shotId, trackId, clipIds)`: Adds PinnedShotGroup to config via applyEdit.
   - `unpinGroup(shotId, trackId)`: Removes PinnedShotGroup from config via applyEdit.
   - `updatePinnedGroup(shotId, trackId, updates)`: Partial update to a pinned group.
2. **Implementation pattern:** Read current `pinnedShotGroups` from `dataRef.current.config`, mutate the array, produce a new config via `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry`, call `applyEdit({ type: 'config', resolvedConfig })`.
3. **Alternative (simpler):** Since pin/unpin also changes rows (for video swap), use `applyEdit({ type: 'rows', ... })` with `rowsToConfig` handling the new `pinnedShotGroups` field. The key insight is that `materializeData` already passes `current.config.pinnedShotGroups` through (from Step 4), so we just need to update the config before calling `applyEdit`.
4. **Decision:** Use a dedicated mutation approach — modify pinnedShotGroups on the config directly and rebuild via `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry`. This keeps pin/unpin operations as pure metadata changes without touching rows.

### Step 10: Update ShotGroupContextMenu (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ShotGroupContextMenu.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `isPinned: boolean` to `ShotGroupMenuState` (line ~4).
2. **Add** `trackId: string` to `ShotGroupMenuState` (needed for pin/unpin targeting).
3. **Add** callback props: `onPinGroup`, `onUnpinGroup`, `onSwitchToImages`.
4. **Add** menu items:
   - "Pin as Shot Group" (when `!isPinned`) — calls `onPinGroup`.
   - "Unpin" (when `isPinned`) — calls `onUnpinGroup`.
   - "Switch to Video" (when `isPinned && mode === 'images'`) — calls `onSwitchToFinalVideo`.
   - "Switch to Images" (when `isPinned && mode === 'video'`) — calls `onSwitchToImages`.
5. **Wire** in TimelineEditor.tsx: pass `usePinnedShotGroups` callbacks and `isPinned` from the group data.

### Step 11: Update context menu opening (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `openShotGroupMenu` calls (line ~783, ~808) to include `isPinned` and `trackId` in the menu state.
2. **Resolve** `trackId` from the group's `rowId` and the track definitions.

## Phase 4: Switch-to-Video Refactor

### Step 12: Refactor useSwitchToFinalVideo for pinned groups (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Auto-pin:** If the group is not pinned, create a PinnedShotGroup entry first.
2. **Snapshot:** Before swapping clips, save image clip metadata into `PinnedShotGroup.imageClipSnapshot` — each entry captures `{ clipId, meta, assetKey }` for restoration.
3. **Swap to video:** Remove image clips, insert single video clip (existing logic). Update PinnedShotGroup: set `mode: 'video'`, `videoAssetKey`, update `clipIds` to `[videoClipId]`.
4. **All as single applyEdit:** Build the full mutation (new rows with video clip + updated pinnedShotGroups in config) and apply atomically.
5. **Add** `switchToImages` function: Read `imageClipSnapshot` from PinnedShotGroup, remove video clip, restore image clips with original meta. Update PinnedShotGroup: set `mode: 'images'`, restore `clipIds`. Single applyEdit.

## Phase 5: Grouped Drag

### Step 13: Implement grouped drag for pinned groups (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **On pointer-down** (line ~100 area): Check if the clicked clip belongs to a pinned group. If so, auto-select all pinned group `clipIds` into `draggedClipIds`.
2. **Build** `clipOffsets` for all group clips (same pattern as existing multi-select drag, line ~381-450).
3. **Leverage** existing `planMultiDragMoves` / `applyMultiDragMoves` infrastructure — no new drag system needed.
4. **Edge case:** If user has manually selected clips that partially overlap a pinned group, expand selection to include all group clips.

## Phase 6: Shot Drop

### Step 14: Handle shot drops from shot list (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Detect** shot-type drag data in `dispatchTimelineDrop` (line ~32). Add a new case for shot drops.
2. **Create handler** `handleShotDrop`: For each image in the shot, create an image clip using `handleAssetDrop` / `registerGenerationAsset` pattern. Calculate sequential timing.
3. **Create PinnedShotGroup:** After clips are created, write a PinnedShotGroup with all new clipIds.
4. **Single applyEdit** for atomic creation of clips + pinned group.

## Phase 7: Auto-Sync

### Step 15: Subscribe to shot image changes for pinned groups
**Scope:** Medium
1. **In TimelineEditor.tsx** or a new `usePinnedGroupSync` hook: Watch `ShotsContext` for changes to shots that have pinned groups on the timeline.
2. **When images are added** to a shot with a pinned group: Create new image clips at the appropriate position in the track, add clipIds to the PinnedShotGroup.
3. **When images are removed:** Remove corresponding clips and update clipIds.
4. **Single applyEdit** for each sync operation.
5. **Guard:** Only sync when `mode === 'images'` (not when showing video).

## Phase 8: Testing

### Step 16: Unit tests (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Verify** all 8 existing tests pass (no changes to test code).
2. **Add** test: pinned group suppresses inferred group for same shot+track.
3. **Add** test: pinned groups appear in output with `isPinned: true`.
4. **Add** test: inferred groups for different shot/track not suppressed by pinned group.
5. **Add** test: mixed pinned + inferred groups merge correctly.

### Step 17: Serialization tests (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** test: config with `pinnedShotGroups` survives `serializeForDisk` round-trip.
2. **Add** test: `validateSerializedConfig` accepts `pinnedShotGroups` key.
3. **Verify** existing round-trip test passes.

### Step 18: Integration smoke tests
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** `npm run build` succeeds (type checking).
2. **Run** full test suite to catch regressions.

## Execution Order
1. Phase 1 (Steps 1–5): Data model and persistence first — everything else depends on this.
2. Phase 2 (Steps 6–8): Inference suppression + visual distinction — makes the feature visible.
3. Phase 3 (Steps 9–11): Context menu — enables user interaction.
4. Phase 4 (Step 12): Switch-to-video refactor — core pinned group behavior.
5. Phase 5 (Step 13): Grouped drag — leverages existing infrastructure.
6. Phase 6 (Step 14): Shot drop — creation path.
7. Phase 7 (Step 15): Auto-sync — reactive behavior.
8. Phase 8 (Steps 16–18): Testing throughout, final verification at end.

## Validation Order
1. After Phase 1: Run serialization tests + existing useShotGroups tests.
2. After Phase 2: Run useShotGroups tests (existing + new). Build check.
3. After Phase 4: Manual verification of switch-to-video/images round-trip.
4. After Phase 8: Full test suite + build.

  "questions": [
    "Should pinned groups survive when clips are manually deleted by the user? (e.g., user deletes 2 of 5 clips in a pinned group \u2014 does the group shrink, or unpin entirely below some threshold?)",
    "For auto-sync (Phase 7): when new images are added to a shot with a pinned group, should the new clips inherit the same duration/timing as existing clips, or should the group expand to accommodate them?",
    "Should 'Switch to Video' be available on suggested groups (auto-pinning first), or only on already-pinned groups?",
    "For shot drops from the shot list: is there an existing drag data format for shots, or does this need a new drag type defined in the shot list/gallery components?",
    "When a pinned group is in video mode and the user manually deletes the video clip, should it auto-switch back to images from snapshot, or just unpin/remove the group?"
  ],
  "success_criteria": [
    {
      "criterion": "All 8 existing useShotGroups tests pass without modification",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "pinnedShotGroups survives full save/load round-trip: serializeForDisk \u2192 validateSerializedConfig \u2192 loadTimeline \u2192 buildTimelineData",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Pinning a suggested group via context menu persists the group in TimelineConfig and it appears with solid border on next render",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Unpinning a pinned group removes it from TimelineConfig and it reverts to dashed suggested border (if still inferable)",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Switch-to-video snapshots image clips and switch-to-images restores them, each as a single applyEdit call",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Undo/redo works for pin, unpin, switch-to-video, and switch-to-images operations",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Dragging any clip in a pinned group moves all group clips together using existing multi-drag infrastructure",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Inferred groups are suppressed when a pinned group exists for the same shot+track combination",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "AI timeline agent (supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/) does not break when pinnedShotGroups is present in config",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "npm run build succeeds with no type errors",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Suggested groups render with dashed border; pinned groups render with solid border using existing color-mix pattern (no hardcoded colors)",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Pinned groups show shot name label always (not just on hover)",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "New usePinnedShotGroups hook is under ~150 lines",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Shot drop from shot list creates pinned group with all shot images as sequential clips",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Auto-sync updates pinned group clips when shot images change via ShotsContext",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Manual smoke test: create suggested group \u2192 pin \u2192 switch to video \u2192 undo \u2192 switch back to images \u2192 unpin \u2192 verify dashed border returns",
      "priority": "info"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Manual smoke test: drag a pinned group across tracks and verify all clips move together",
      "priority": "info"
    }
  ],
  "assumptions": [
    "Pinned groups with fewer than 1 clip remaining (after manual deletion) should be auto-removed from config. Groups with 1+ clips survive.",
    "Switch-to-Video is available on suggested groups (auto-pins first, as stated in the idea doc). This keeps the UX simple \u2014 users don't need to learn to pin before switching.",
    "The existing drag data format does not include shot-type drags. A new drag type will need to be defined, likely originating from the shot list/gallery components. This is Phase 6 and can be deferred if the shot list doesn't support drag yet.",
    "Auto-sync (Phase 7) will match new image clip duration to the average duration of existing clips in the pinned group.",
    "repairConfig and migrateToFlatTracks in timeline-data.ts/save-utils.ts pass through unknown config keys (they only touch output/clips/tracks). Will verify during implementation and add explicit passthrough if needed.",
    "The AI timeline agent reads TimelineConfig but only uses output/clips/tracks fields. Adding pinnedShotGroups will not break it because it ignores unknown keys. Will verify in Step 5.",
    "imageClipSnapshot stores enough data (clipId, meta, assetKey) to fully restore image clips without re-fetching from the server."
  ],

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"

        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"

      },

        ### Suggested Approach
        **Phase 1: Data model** — Add `PinnedShotGroup` type and `pinnedShotGroups?: PinnedShotGroup[]` to `TimelineConfig` (types/index.ts), `TimelineData` (timeline-data.ts). Update serialize.ts: add to `ALLOWED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS`, pass through in `serializeForDisk`, allow in `validateSerializedConfig`. Thread through `assembleTimelineData`, `buildTimelineData`, `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry`, and `rowsToConfig`.

**Phase 2: Inference suppression** — Modify `useShotGroups` to accept `pinnedShotGroups` param. Build a Set of `${shotId}:${trackId}` keys from pinned groups. Skip inferred groups matching those keys. Add `isPinned: boolean` to `ShotGroup` interface so downstream code can distinguish. Return merged array: pinned groups (from config) + filtered inferred groups.

**Phase 3: Visual distinction** — Add `isPinned` to `PositionedShotGroup` in TimelineCanvas. Render dashed border (`border-dashed`) for suggested, solid for pinned. Show shot name label always for pinned (not just on hover). Keep existing color-mix pattern.

**Phase 4: Pin/Unpin via context menu** — Add `isPinned` to `ShotGroupMenuState`. Add "Pin as Shot Group" / "Unpin" menu items. Pin action: read current clipIds from the inferred group, write `PinnedShotGroup` to config via `applyEdit`. Unpin: remove from config via `applyEdit`. Create a `usePinnedShotGroups` hook encapsulating pin/unpin/update mutations.

**Phase 5: Switch-to-video refactor** — Modify `useSwitchToFinalVideo` to auto-pin first if not already pinned. Before swapping, snapshot image clip metas into `PinnedShotGroup.imageClipSnapshot`. On switch-back, restore from snapshot. Both directions as single `applyEdit` calls.

**Phase 6: Grouped drag** — In `useClipDrag`, on pointer-down check if clip belongs to a pinned group. If so, auto-select all pinned group clipIds into `draggedClipIds`. This leverages existing multi-drag path with `planMultiDragMoves`/`applyMultiDragMoves`.

**Phase 7: Shot drop** — In `useExternalDrop`, detect shot-type drag data. On drop, create image clips for all shot images using `handleAssetDrop` pattern, then write a `PinnedShotGroup` entry. Single `applyEdit` for atomic creation.

**Phase 8: Auto-sync** — Subscribe to `ShotsContext` changes. When images added/removed from a shot that has a pinned group, update the pinned group's clipIds and add/remove corresponding timeline clips via `applyEdit`.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Implement two-tier shot groups on the video editor timeline: suggested (inferred, dashed border) and pinned (persistent, solid border).

## Core concept
The current useShotGroups inference becomes 'suggested' groups shown with dashed borders. Users can promote them to 'pinned' groups which are stored in timeline config, persist across refresh, and enable advanced features.

## Data model
PinnedShotGroup stored in timelineConfig:
- shotId, trackId, clipIds[], mode: 'images' | 'video', videoAssetKey?, imageClipSnapshot? (for swap-back)

## When pinned groups are created (explicit + implicit):

### Explicit:
1. Right-click a suggested group → 'Pin as Shot Group' - writes PinnedShotGroup to config
2. Drop a shot from shot list/gallery onto timeline - creates pinned group with images expanded as clips

### Implicit (auto-pin):
3. 'Switch to Final Video' on a suggested group - auto-pins first (snapshots image clips), then swaps to video mode
4. When an image is added to a shot that already has a pinned group on the timeline - auto-inserts the new image clip into the pinned group's track at the right position

## Visual distinction:
- Suggested: dashed border, lighter color (current border style → dashed)
- Pinned: solid border, full color (current styling), shot name visible

## Pinned group behavior:
- Clips drag together as a unit (grouped drag)
- Image ↔ video toggle: switch stores snapshot of image clips, restores on switch-back. Single applyEdit each direction.
- Shot image sync: when images are added/removed from a shot, pinned group auto-updates (subscribe to shot changes via realtime or ShotsContext)
- Context menu: Pin/Unpin, Switch to Video/Images, Jump to Shot, Generate Video

## Key architectural decisions:
- pinnedShotGroups stored in TimelineData (persisted via timeline config, undoable)
- useShotGroups still runs for inference but pinned groups take priority (suppress inferred for same shot+track)
- PinnedShotGroup clipIds are the source of truth for which clips belong to the group
- When a pinned group exists, the shot group overlay reads from the pinned data, not from inference

## Key files to research:
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.ts - current inference
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts - current swap (to be replaced)
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts - applyEdit, TimelineEditMutation, timeline config persistence
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts - drag behavior (need grouped drag)
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts - drop from outside (need shot drop)
- src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts - TimelineData type, ClipMeta
- src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts - AssetRegistry types
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx - rendering shot groups
- src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ShotGroupContextMenu.tsx - context menu
- src/shared/contexts/ShotsContext.tsx - shot data, realtime updates
- src/shared/hooks/shots/useShotImages.ts - shot image data
- src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useAssetManagement.ts - asset registration
- src/tools/video-editor/contexts/TimelineEditorContext.ts - editor context/providers

User notes and answers:
- Addressing critique flags:

FLAG-001 (suppression granularity): Use clipIds-based suppression instead of shotId:trackId. A pinned group stores its clipIds. When filtering inferred groups, suppress any inferred group whose clipIds are a subset of a pinned group's clipIds. This handles multiple clusters of the same shot on one track.

FLAG-002/correctness-2/callers-1 (applyEdit config mutations): Add a new mutation type to TimelineEditMutation: { type: 'pinnedShotGroups', pinnedShotGroups: PinnedShotGroup[] }. This is cleaner than trying to squeeze metadata into the existing config or rows paths. The commit handler reads current data, replaces pinnedShotGroups, and rebuilds. For switch-to-video which needs BOTH rows changes AND pinnedShotGroups changes, use the existing type:'rows' mutation but add an optional pinnedShotGroupsOverride field.

FLAG-003 (grouped drag): Defer group expansion to after drag threshold is crossed, not on pointer-down. On pointer-down, just record that the clip is in a pinned group. On first pointermove past threshold, expand draggedClipIds to include all group clips.

FLAG-004/scope-1/all_locations-3 (shot drop DnD): Add a new drag type 'shot' to shared DnD utilities. Add drag source in shot list components. Add acceptance in onTimelineDragOver. This is Phase 6 and can be a follow-up if needed.

FLAG-005/all_locations-1 (migrateToFlatTracks): Explicitly update migrateToFlatTracks to preserve pinnedShotGroups. It currently rebuilds config with an allowlist - add pinnedShotGroups to that list.

all_locations-2 (rowsToConfig callers): Update render-bounds.validation.test.ts and any other direct callers.

issue_hints-1 (clip lifecycle): When a clip in a pinned group is manually deleted, remove it from the group's clipIds. If clipIds becomes empty, remove the pinned group. If in video mode and the video clip is deleted, auto-switch back to images from snapshot if available, otherwise remove the group.

issue_hints-2 (hasFinalVideo guard): Preserve the existing guard - Switch to Video only shows when finalVideoMap.get(shotId) exists. Switch to Images only shows when mode is video.

conventions-1 (reuse insertion helpers): Reuse useAssetManagement helpers for shot drop clip creation rather than hand-building.

conventions-2 (mutation model): All pinned group changes go through applyEdit, never direct mutation of dataRef.current. This preserves undo/history.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Two-Tier Shot Groups (Suggested + Pinned)

## Overview

Add a two-tier shot group system to the video editor timeline. The existing `useShotGroups` inference becomes "suggested" groups (dashed border). Users can promote these to "pinned" groups persisted in `TimelineConfig`, enabling grouped drag, image↔video toggle with snapshot/restore, auto-sync with shot image changes, and context menu actions.

**Critical architectural constraint:** The edit/commit cycle reconstructs `TimelineData` from scratch via `rowsToConfig → buildDataFromCurrentRegistry → assembleTimelineData`. Additionally, `migrateToFlatTracks()` in `migrate.ts:167-186` rebuilds config with an explicit allowlist of only `{output, tracks, clips, customEffects}`. All these functions must explicitly preserve `pinnedShotGroups`, following the `customEffects` pattern.

**Mutation model:** A new `TimelineEditMutation` variant `{ type: 'pinnedShotGroups' }` handles metadata-only pin/unpin. For operations that change both rows and pinned groups (switch-to-video), the existing `type: 'rows'` mutation gains an optional `pinnedShotGroupsOverride` field. This avoids overloading the existing `type: 'config'` contract which is used by track management, clip editing, and multi-drag callers.

**Suppression strategy:** Inferred groups are suppressed using clipIds-based subset matching (not shotId:trackId), so multiple inferred clusters of the same shot on one track are handled correctly — only the cluster whose clipIds are a subset of a pinned group's clipIds is suppressed.

## Phase 1: Data Model & Persistence

### Step 1: Define PinnedShotGroup type and add to TimelineConfig (`src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `PinnedShotGroup` interface:
   ```typescript
   export interface PinnedShotGroup {
     shotId: string;
     trackId: string;
     clipIds: string[];
     mode: 'images' | 'video';
     videoAssetKey?: string;
     imageClipSnapshot?: Array<{ clipId: string; meta: ClipMeta; assetKey: string }>;
   }
   ```
2. **Add** optional `pinnedShotGroups?: PinnedShotGroup[]` to `TimelineConfig` type (~line 146).

### Step 2: Add new mutation type to TimelineEditMutation (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend** `TimelineEditMutation` (~line 46) with a third variant:
   ```typescript
   | { type: 'pinnedShotGroups'; pinnedShotGroups: PinnedShotGroup[] }
   ```
2. **Add** optional `pinnedShotGroupsOverride?: PinnedShotGroup[]` to the `type: 'rows'` variant, for operations that change both rows and pinned groups atomically (switch-to-video/images).
3. **Implement** the `'pinnedShotGroups'` branch in `applyEdit` (~line 184): read `current` from `dataRef`, replace `config.pinnedShotGroups`, call `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry(updatedConfig, current)`, then `commitData()`.
4. **Update** the `'rows'` branch in `applyEdit` (~line 193): after `materializeData()`, if `mutation.pinnedShotGroupsOverride` is set, overwrite `pinnedShotGroups` on the resulting config before `commitData()`.

### Step 3: Thread pinnedShotGroups through serialization (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `'pinnedShotGroups'` to `ALLOWED_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS` set (line 98).
2. **Update** `serializeForDisk` (~line 131): conditionally include `pinnedShotGroups` in the serialized output, following the `customEffects` pattern.

### Step 4: Thread pinnedShotGroups through migration (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/migrate.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `migrateToFlatTracks` (lines 167-186): both return paths must include `pinnedShotGroups: config.pinnedShotGroups` in the constructed config object. This is a required edit — the function rebuilds config with an allowlist and currently drops unknown keys.

### Step 5: Thread pinnedShotGroups through data assembly (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** `rowsToConfig` (~line 300): Accept optional `pinnedShotGroups` parameter. Include it in the returned `TimelineConfig` if non-empty (same pattern as `customEffects`).
2. **No change** to `assembleTimelineData` — it receives the full `config` object which now includes `pinnedShotGroups`. The field flows through `config` naturally.
3. **No change** to `configToRows` — `pinnedShotGroups` is metadata, not clip data.

### Step 6: Thread through commit cycle (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-save-utils.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `materializeData` (~line 114): pass `current.config.pinnedShotGroups` as the new `pinnedShotGroups` argument to `rowsToConfig()`.
2. **Verify** `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry` (~line 23): the `config` parameter already has `pinnedShotGroups` as part of `TimelineConfig`. It passes through `repairConfig()` → `migrateToFlatTracks()` (fixed in Step 4) → `assembleTimelineData()`. No additional changes needed here.

### Step 7: Update rowsToConfig callers and tests
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `render-bounds.validation.test.ts:125`: add the new optional `pinnedShotGroups` parameter (pass `undefined` since the test doesn't exercise pinned groups).
2. **Search** for any other direct `rowsToConfig` callers and update their call sites.

### Step 8: Verify persistence round-trip
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** existing serialization tests: `src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.test.ts`.
2. **Add** a test case that includes `pinnedShotGroups` in a config and verifies it survives `serializeForDisk → validateSerializedConfig` round-trip.
3. **AI timeline agent compatibility:** Verified — `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/db.ts` uses permissive `as TimelineConfig` casting without field validation. Extra keys pass through without error.

## Phase 2: Inference Suppression & Visual Distinction

### Step 9: Update useShotGroups to support pinned groups (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `isPinned: boolean` field to `ShotGroup` interface (~line 10).
2. **Add** optional `pinnedShotGroups?: PinnedShotGroup[]` parameter to `useShotGroups` function signature (~line 39).
3. **Build** a Set of pinned clipId sets from `pinnedShotGroups` for subset checking.
4. **Convert** each `PinnedShotGroup` into a `ShotGroup` with `isPinned: true`, using `getShotColor(shotId)` for color and resolving `shotName` from shots array.
5. **Filter** inferred groups: suppress any inferred group whose `clipIds` are a subset of any pinned group's `clipIds`. This correctly handles multiple separate inferred clusters of the same shot on one track — only the cluster that overlaps the pinned group is suppressed.
6. **Return** merged array: pinned ShotGroups + filtered inferred ShotGroups (all inferred have `isPinned: false`).
7. **Verify** all 8 existing tests pass unchanged (they don't pass `pinnedShotGroups`, so the parameter defaults to `undefined` and behavior is identical).

### Step 10: Wire pinnedShotGroups from TimelineData into useShotGroups (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `useShotGroups` call (~line 327) to pass `data?.config.pinnedShotGroups` as the new parameter.

### Step 11: Visual distinction in TimelineCanvas (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `isPinned: boolean` to `PositionedShotGroup` interface (~line 60).
2. **Populate** `isPinned` from `ShotGroup.isPinned` in the `positionedShotGroups` useMemo (~line 415).
3. **Update** shot group border rendering (~lines 758-769):
   - Suggested: add `border-dashed` class. Keep current `color-mix()` opacity.
   - Pinned: keep `border-solid` (current default) with full color. No hardcoded colors.
4. **Show** shot name label always for pinned groups (not just on hover). For suggested groups, keep hover behavior.

## Phase 3: Context Menu & Pin/Unpin

### Step 12: Create usePinnedShotGroups hook (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** hook that encapsulates pin/unpin mutations via `applyEdit`:
   - `pinGroup(shotId, trackId, clipIds)`: Reads current `pinnedShotGroups` from `dataRef.current.config`, appends new `PinnedShotGroup` with `mode: 'images'`, calls `applyEdit({ type: 'pinnedShotGroups', pinnedShotGroups: [...current, newGroup] })`.
   - `unpinGroup(shotId, trackId)`: Filters out matching group, calls `applyEdit({ type: 'pinnedShotGroups', ... })`.
   - `updatePinnedGroup(shotId, trackId, updates)`: Partial update to a pinned group via same mutation type.
2. All mutations go through `applyEdit`, never direct mutation of `dataRef.current`. This preserves undo/redo history.

### Step 13: Update ShotGroupContextMenu (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ShotGroupContextMenu.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `isPinned: boolean` and `trackId: string` to `ShotGroupMenuState` (~line 4).
2. **Add** `mode?: 'images' | 'video'` to `ShotGroupMenuState` for pinned groups.
3. **Add** callback props: `onPinGroup`, `onUnpinGroup`, `onSwitchToImages`.
4. **Add** menu items:
   - "Pin as Shot Group" (when `!isPinned`) — calls `onPinGroup`.
   - "Unpin" (when `isPinned`) — calls `onUnpinGroup`.
   - "Switch to Final Video" (when `hasFinalVideo && (!isPinned || mode === 'images')`) — preserves existing `finalVideoMap.get(shotId)` availability guard. Calls existing `onSwitchToFinalVideo`.
   - "Switch to Images" (when `isPinned && mode === 'video'`) — calls `onSwitchToImages`.
5. **Preserve** existing `onShotGroupSwitchToFinalVideo` callback signature `{ shotId, clipIds, rowId }` and the `finalVideo.id` dismissal flow in TimelineEditor.

### Step 14: Update context menu opening (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `openShotGroupMenu` calls (~lines 783, 808) to include `isPinned`, `trackId`, and `mode` in the menu state.
2. **Resolve** `trackId` from the group's `rowId` and the track definitions.

### Step 15: Wire callbacks in TimelineEditor (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Call** `usePinnedShotGroups` hook, pass `pinGroup`/`unpinGroup` callbacks to `ShotGroupContextMenu`.
2. **Pass** `isPinned` and `mode` data through to the context menu state.

## Phase 4: Clip Lifecycle & Switch-to-Video Refactor

### Step 16: Handle clip deletion in pinned groups
**Scope:** Medium
1. **After each `applyEdit` commit** (in `commitData` or as a post-commit effect): compare pinned group `clipIds` against the actual clips in `data.rows`. Remove any `clipIds` that no longer exist.
2. **If `clipIds` becomes empty:** remove the pinned group from config.
3. **If in video mode and the video clip is deleted:** check for `imageClipSnapshot`. If available, restore image clips via `applyEdit({ type: 'rows', pinnedShotGroupsOverride })`. If no snapshot, remove the group.
4. **Implementation:** Add a `reconcilePinnedGroups` utility called during the commit cycle that detects stale clipIds and produces a cleaned pinnedShotGroups array.

### Step 17: Refactor useSwitchToFinalVideo for pinned groups (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Auto-pin:** If the group is not already pinned, create a `PinnedShotGroup` entry with `mode: 'images'`.
2. **Snapshot:** Before swapping clips, save image clip metadata into `PinnedShotGroup.imageClipSnapshot` — each entry captures `{ clipId, meta, assetKey }` for restoration.
3. **Swap to video:** Remove image clips, insert single video clip (existing logic). Update PinnedShotGroup: set `mode: 'video'`, `videoAssetKey`, update `clipIds` to `[videoClipId]`.
4. **Use `applyEdit({ type: 'rows', pinnedShotGroupsOverride })`** — a single call that atomically changes both the rows and the pinned group metadata. Clean undo in one step.
5. **Preserve** existing guard: "Switch to Video" only available when `finalVideoMap.get(shotId)` returns a value. Preserve the `finalVideo.id` dismissal flow.
6. **Add** `switchToImages` function: Read `imageClipSnapshot` from PinnedShotGroup, remove video clip, restore image clips with original meta. Update PinnedShotGroup: set `mode: 'images'`, restore `clipIds`. Single `applyEdit({ type: 'rows', pinnedShotGroupsOverride })`.

## Phase 5: Grouped Drag

### Step 18: Implement grouped drag for pinned groups (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **On pointer-down** (~line 100): record whether the clicked clip belongs to a pinned group (store group reference in session or a ref). Do NOT expand `draggedClipIds` yet — this preserves normal click-selection semantics.
2. **On first pointermove past `DRAG_THRESHOLD_PX`** (~line 250): if the clip is in a pinned group, expand `draggedClipIds` to include all pinned group clipIds. Build `clipOffsets` for all group clips.
3. **Leverage** existing `planMultiDragMoves` / `applyMultiDragMoves` infrastructure (~lines 381-450) — no new drag system needed.
4. **Edge case:** If user has manually selected clips that partially overlap a pinned group, expand selection to include all group clips on drag start.

## Phase 6: Shot Drop (deferrable follow-up)

### Step 19: Add 'shot' drag type to shared DnD utilities (`src/shared/lib/dnd/dragDrop.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `'shot'` to `DragType` union (line 60): `'generation' | 'generation-multi' | 'file' | 'shot' | 'none'`.
2. **Add** `ShotDropData` interface: `{ shotId: string; shotName: string; imageGenerationIds: string[] }`.
3. **Add** `setShotDragData(e, data)` and `getShotDropData(e)` functions following existing `setGenerationDragData`/`getGenerationDropData` pattern with a new MIME type `application/x-shot`.
4. **Update** `getDragType()` to detect the new MIME type.

### Step 20: Add drag source in shot list components
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Find** shot list/gallery components that render individual shots.
2. **Add** `draggable` attribute and `onDragStart` handler that calls `setShotDragData()`.
3. **Add** drag preview using existing `createDragPreview()`.

### Step 21: Handle shot drops on timeline (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** `onTimelineDragOver` to accept `'shot'` drag type (currently rejects anything not in its allowlist).
2. **Add** `handleShotDrop` handler in `dispatchTimelineDrop` (~line 32): For each image in the shot, create an image clip using existing `registerGenerationAsset` / asset management helpers from `useAssetManagement`.
3. **Create** `PinnedShotGroup` with all new clipIds, `mode: 'images'`.
4. **Use** single `applyEdit({ type: 'rows', pinnedShotGroupsOverride })` for atomic creation of clips + pinned group.
5. **Update** `useExternalDrop.test.tsx` with test cases for shot drag type acceptance.

## Phase 7: Auto-Sync

### Step 22: Subscribe to shot image changes for pinned groups
**Scope:** Medium
1. **In** TimelineEditor.tsx or a new `usePinnedGroupSync` hook: Watch `ShotsContext` for changes to shots that have pinned groups on the timeline.
2. **When images are added** to a shot with a pinned group in `mode: 'images'`: Create new image clips at the appropriate position in the track, add clipIds to the PinnedShotGroup. Match duration to average of existing clips.
3. **When images are removed:** Remove corresponding clips and update clipIds.
4. **Use** `applyEdit({ type: 'rows', pinnedShotGroupsOverride })` for each sync operation.
5. **Guard:** Only sync when `mode === 'images'` (not when showing video).

## Phase 8: Testing & Validation

### Step 23: Unit tests for useShotGroups (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Verify** all 8 existing tests pass without modification.
2. **Add** test: pinned group suppresses inferred group whose clipIds are a subset.
3. **Add** test: pinned group does NOT suppress inferred group for same shot but different clipIds (separate cluster on same track).
4. **Add** test: pinned groups appear in output with `isPinned: true`.
5. **Add** test: mixed pinned + inferred groups merge correctly.

### Step 24: Serialization tests (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/serialize.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** test: config with `pinnedShotGroups` survives `serializeForDisk` round-trip.
2. **Add** test: `validateSerializedConfig` accepts `pinnedShotGroups` key.
3. **Verify** existing round-trip test passes.

### Step 25: Build and full test suite
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `npm run build` — must succeed with no type errors.
2. **Run** full test suite to catch regressions.

## Execution Order
1. **Phase 1 (Steps 1–8):** Data model and persistence. Everything else depends on this. Run serialization + useShotGroups tests after.
2. **Phase 2 (Steps 9–11):** Inference suppression + visual distinction. Run useShotGroups tests + build check.
3. **Phase 3 (Steps 12–15):** Context menu and pin/unpin. Build check.
4. **Phase 4 (Steps 16–17):** Clip lifecycle + switch-to-video refactor. Manual verification of undo round-trip.
5. **Phase 5 (Step 18):** Grouped drag.
6. **Phase 6 (Steps 19–21):** Shot drop — can be deferred as a follow-up if shot list doesn't currently support drag.
7. **Phase 7 (Step 22):** Auto-sync — reactive behavior, can be deferred.
8. **Phase 8 (Steps 23–25):** Testing throughout, final verification at end.

## Validation Order
1. After Phase 1: Run `serialize.test.ts` + `useShotGroups.test.ts` + `render-bounds.validation.test.ts`.
2. After Phase 2: Run `useShotGroups.test.ts` (existing + new). `npm run build`.
3. After Phase 4: Manual verification — pin → switch to video → undo → switch to images → unpin.
4. After Phase 8: Full `npm run build` + full test suite.

Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.
